#### `duckdb_create_copy_function`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Creates a new empty copy function.

The return value must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_copy_function`.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_copy_function duckdb_create_copy_function(void);
```

##### Return Value

The copy function object.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_set_name`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the name of the copy function.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_set_name(
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function,
  const char *name
);
```

##### Parameters

* `copy_function`: The copy function
* `name`: The name to set

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_set_extra_info`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the extra info pointer of the copy function, which can be used to store arbitrary data.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_set_extra_info(
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function,
  void *extra_info,
  duckdb_delete_callback_t destructor
);
```

##### Parameters

* `copy_function`: The copy function
* `extra_info`: The extra info pointer
* `destructor`: A destructor function to call to destroy the extra info

<br>

#### `duckdb_register_copy_function`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Registers the given copy function on the database connection under the specified name.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_register_copy_function(
  duckdb_connection connection,
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function
);
```

##### Parameters

* `connection`: The database connection
* `copy_function`: The copy function to register

##### Return Value

`duckdb_state`

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_copy_function`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the given copy function object.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_copy_function(
  duckdb_copy_function *copy_function
);
```

##### Parameters

* `copy_function`: The copy function to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_set_bind`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the bind function of the copy function, to use when binding `COPY ... TO`.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_set_bind(
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function,
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_t bind
);
```

##### Parameters

* `bind`: The bind function

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_bind_set_error`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Report that an error occurred during the binding-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_bind_set_error(
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_info info,
  const char *error
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The bind info provided to the bind function
* `error`: The error message

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_extra_info`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the extra info pointer of the copy function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_extra_info(
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The bind info provided to the bind function

##### Return Value

The extra info pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_client_context`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the client context of the current connection binding the `COPY ... TO` function.

Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_client_context`

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_client_context duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_client_context(
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The bind info provided to the bind function

##### Return Value

The client context.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_column_count`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the number of columns that will be provided to the `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
idx_t duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_column_count(
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The bind info provided to the bind function

##### Return Value

The number of columns.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_column_type`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the type of a column that will be provided to the `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_logical_type duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_column_type(
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_info info,
  idx_t col_idx
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The bind info provided to the bind function
* `col_idx`: The index of the column to retrieve the type for

##### Return Value

The type of the column. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_logical_type`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_options`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves all values for the given options provided to the `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_value duckdb_copy_function_bind_get_options(
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The bind info provided to the bind function

##### Return Value

A STRUCT value containing all options as fields. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_value`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_bind_set_bind_data`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the bind data of the copy function, to be provided to the init, sink and finalize functions.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_bind_set_bind_data(
  duckdb_copy_function_bind_info info,
  void *bind_data,
  duckdb_delete_callback_t destructor
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The bind info provided to the bind function
* `bind_data`: The bind data pointer
* `destructor`: A destructor function to call to destroy the bind data

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_set_global_init`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the initialization function of the copy function, called right before executing `COPY ... TO`.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_set_global_init(
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function,
  duckdb_copy_function_global_init_t init
);
```

##### Parameters

* `init`: The init function

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_global_init_set_error`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Report that an error occurred during the initialization-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_global_init_set_error(
  duckdb_copy_function_global_init_info info,
  const char *error
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The init info provided to the init function
* `error`: The error message

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_extra_info`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the extra info pointer of the copy function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_extra_info(
  duckdb_copy_function_global_init_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The init info provided to the init function

##### Return Value

The extra info pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_client_context`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the client context of the current connection initializing the `COPY ... TO` function.

Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_client_context`

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_client_context duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_client_context(
  duckdb_copy_function_global_init_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The init info provided to the init function

##### Return Value

The client context.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_bind_data`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the bind data provided during the binding-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_bind_data(
  duckdb_copy_function_global_init_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The init info provided to the init function

##### Return Value

The bind data pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_file_path`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the file path provided to the `COPY ... TO` function.

Lives for the duration of the initialization callback, must not be destroyed.

##### Syntax

```c
const char *duckdb_copy_function_global_init_get_file_path(
  duckdb_copy_function_global_init_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The init info provided to the init function

##### Return Value

The file path.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_global_init_set_global_state`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the global state of the copy function, to be provided to all subsequent local init, sink and finalize functions.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_global_init_set_global_state(
  duckdb_copy_function_global_init_info info,
  void *global_state,
  duckdb_delete_callback_t destructor
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The init info provided to the init function
* `global_state`: The global state pointer
* `destructor`: A destructor function to call to destroy the global state

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_set_sink`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the sink function of the copy function, called during `COPY ... TO`.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_set_sink(
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function,
  duckdb_copy_function_sink_t function
);
```

##### Parameters

* `function`: The sink function

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_sink_set_error`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Report that an error occurred during the sink-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_sink_set_error(
  duckdb_copy_function_sink_info info,
  const char *error
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The sink info provided to the sink function
* `error`: The error message

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_extra_info`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the extra info pointer of the copy function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_extra_info(
  duckdb_copy_function_sink_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The sink info provided to the sink function

##### Return Value

The extra info pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_client_context`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the client context of the current connection during the sink-phase of the `COPY ... TO` function.

Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_client_context`

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_client_context duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_client_context(
  duckdb_copy_function_sink_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The sink info provided to the sink function

##### Return Value

The client context.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_bind_data`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the bind data provided during the binding-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_bind_data(
  duckdb_copy_function_sink_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The sink info provided to the sink function

##### Return Value

The bind data pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_global_state`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the global state provided during the init-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_sink_get_global_state(
  duckdb_copy_function_sink_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The sink info provided to the sink function

##### Return Value

The global state pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_set_finalize`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the finalize function of the copy function, called at the end of `COPY ... TO`.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_set_finalize(
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function,
  duckdb_copy_function_finalize_t finalize
);
```

##### Parameters

* `finalize`: The finalize function

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_finalize_set_error`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Report that an error occurred during the finalize-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_finalize_set_error(
  duckdb_copy_function_finalize_info info,
  const char *error
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The finalize info provided to the finalize function
* `error`: The error message

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_extra_info`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the extra info pointer of the copy function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_extra_info(
  duckdb_copy_function_finalize_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The finalize info provided to the finalize function

##### Return Value

The extra info pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_client_context`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the client context of the current connection during the finalize-phase of the `COPY ... TO` function.

Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_client_context`

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_client_context duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_client_context(
  duckdb_copy_function_finalize_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The finalize info provided to the finalize function

##### Return Value

The client context.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_bind_data`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the bind data provided during the binding-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_bind_data(
  duckdb_copy_function_finalize_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The finalize info provided to the finalize function

##### Return Value

The bind data pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_global_state`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the global state provided during the init-phase of a `COPY ... TO` function.

##### Syntax

```c
void *duckdb_copy_function_finalize_get_global_state(
  duckdb_copy_function_finalize_info info
);
```

##### Parameters

* `info`: The finalize info provided to the finalize function

##### Return Value

The global state pointer.

<br>

#### `duckdb_copy_function_set_copy_from_function`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the table function to use when executing a `COPY ... FROM (...)` statement with this copy function.

The table function must have a `duckdb_table_function_bind_t`, `duckdb_table_function_init_t` and `duckdb_table_function_t` set.

The table function must take a single VARCHAR parameter (the file path).

Options passed to the `COPY ... FROM (...)` statement are forwarded as named parameters to the table function.

Since `COPY ... FROM` copies into an already existing table, the table function should not define its own result columns using `duckdb_bind_add_result_column` when binding
. Instead use `duckdb_table_function_bind_get_result_column_count` and related functions in the bind callback of the table function to retrieve the schema of the target table of the `COPY ... FROM` statement.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_copy_function_set_copy_from_function(
  duckdb_copy_function copy_function,
  duckdb_table_function table_function
);
```

##### Parameters

* `copy_function`: The copy function
* `table_function`: The table function to use for `COPY ... FROM`

<br>

<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_query_arrow_schema"><span class="nf">duckdb_query_arrow_schema</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> <span class="nv">result</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_schema</span> *<span class="nv">out_schema</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_prepared_arrow_schema"><span class="nf">duckdb_prepared_arrow_schema</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_prepared_statement</span> <span class="nv">prepared</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_schema</span> *<span class="nv">out_schema</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_result_arrow_array"><span class="nf">duckdb_result_arrow_array</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_result</span> <span class="nv">result</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_data_chunk</span> <span class="nv">chunk</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_array</span> *<span class="nv">out_array</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_query_arrow_array"><span class="nf">duckdb_query_arrow_array</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> <span class="nv">result</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_array</span> *<span class="nv">out_array</span>);
<span class="kt">idx_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_arrow_column_count"><span class="nf">duckdb_arrow_column_count</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> <span class="nv">result</span>);
<span class="kt">idx_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_arrow_row_count"><span class="nf">duckdb_arrow_row_count</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> <span class="nv">result</span>);
<span class="kt">idx_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_arrow_rows_changed"><span class="nf">duckdb_arrow_rows_changed</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> <span class="nv">result</span>);
<span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<a href="#duckdb_query_arrow_error"><span class="nf">duckdb_query_arrow_error</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> <span class="nv">result</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_arrow"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_arrow</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> *<span class="nv">result</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_arrow_stream"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_arrow_stream</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_stream</span> *<span class="nv">stream_p</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_execute_prepared_arrow"><span class="nf">duckdb_execute_prepared_arrow</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_prepared_statement</span> <span class="nv">prepared_statement</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow</span> *<span class="nv">out_result</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_arrow_scan"><span class="nf">duckdb_arrow_scan</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_connection</span> <span class="nv">connection</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<span class="nv">table_name</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_stream</span> <span class="nv">arrow</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_arrow_array_scan"><span class="nf">duckdb_arrow_array_scan</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_connection</span> <span class="nv">connection</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<span class="nv">table_name</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_schema</span> <span class="nv">arrow_schema</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_array</span> <span class="nv">arrow_array</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_arrow_stream</span> *<span class="nv">out_stream</span>);
</code></pre></div></div>

#### `duckdb_to_arrow_schema`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Transforms a DuckDB Schema into an Arrow Schema

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_error_data duckdb_to_arrow_schema(
  duckdb_arrow_options arrow_options,
  duckdb_logical_type *types,
  const char **names,
  idx_t column_count,
  struct ArrowSchema *out_schema
);
```

##### Parameters

* `arrow_options`: The Arrow settings used to produce arrow.
* `types`: The DuckDB logical types for each column in the schema.
* `names`: The names for each column in the schema.
* `column_count`: The number of columns that exist in the schema.
* `out_schema`: The resulting arrow schema. Must be destroyed with `out_schema->release(out_schema)`. (out)

##### Return Value

The error data. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_error_data`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_data_chunk_to_arrow`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Transforms a DuckDB data chunk into an Arrow array.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_error_data duckdb_data_chunk_to_arrow(
  duckdb_arrow_options arrow_options,
  duckdb_data_chunk chunk,
  struct ArrowArray *out_arrow_array
);
```

##### Parameters

* `arrow_options`: The Arrow settings used to produce arrow.
* `chunk`: The DuckDB data chunk to convert.
* `out_arrow_array`: The output Arrow structure that will hold the converted data. Must be released with `out_arrow_array->release(out_arrow_array)` (out)

##### Return Value

The error data. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_error_data`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_schema_from_arrow`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Transforms an Arrow Schema into a DuckDB Schema.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_error_data duckdb_schema_from_arrow(
  duckdb_connection connection,
  struct ArrowSchema *schema,
  duckdb_arrow_converted_schema *out_types
);
```

##### Parameters

* `connection`: The connection to get the transformation settings from.
* `schema`: The input Arrow schema. Must be released with `schema->release(schema)`.
* `out_types`: The Arrow converted schema with extra information about the arrow types. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_arrow_converted_schema`. (out)

##### Return Value

The error data. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_error_data`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_data_chunk_from_arrow`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Transforms an Arrow array into a DuckDB data chunk. The data chunk will retain ownership of the underlying Arrow data.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_error_data duckdb_data_chunk_from_arrow(
  duckdb_connection connection,
  struct ArrowArray *arrow_array,
  duckdb_arrow_converted_schema converted_schema,
  duckdb_data_chunk *out_chunk
);
```

##### Parameters

* `connection`: The connection to get the transformation settings from.
* `arrow_array`: The input Arrow array. Data ownership is passed on to DuckDB's DataChunk, the underlying object does not need to be released and won't have ownership of the data.
* `converted_schema`: The Arrow converted schema with extra information about the arrow types.
* `out_chunk`: The resulting DuckDB data chunk. Must be destroyed by duckdb_destroy_data_chunk. (out)

##### Return Value

The error data. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_error_data`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_arrow_converted_schema`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the arrow converted schema and de-allocates all memory allocated for that arrow converted schema.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_arrow_converted_schema(
  duckdb_arrow_converted_schema *arrow_converted_schema
);
```

##### Parameters

* `arrow_converted_schema`: The arrow converted schema to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_query_arrow`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Executes a SQL query within a connection and stores the full (materialized) result in an arrow structure.
If the query fails to execute, DuckDBError is returned and the error message can be retrieved by calling
`duckdb_query_arrow_error`.

Note that after running `duckdb_query_arrow`, `duckdb_destroy_arrow` must be called on the result object even if the
query fails, otherwise the error stored within the result will not be freed correctly.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_query_arrow(
  duckdb_connection connection,
  const char *query,
  duckdb_arrow *out_result
);
```

##### Parameters

* `connection`: The connection to perform the query in.
* `query`: The SQL query to run.
* `out_result`: The query result. (out)

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure.

<br>

#### `duckdb_query_arrow_schema`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Fetch the internal arrow schema from the arrow result. Remember to call release on the respective
ArrowSchema object.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_query_arrow_schema(
  duckdb_arrow result,
  duckdb_arrow_schema *out_schema
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result to fetch the schema from.
* `out_schema`: The output schema. (out)

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure.

<br>

#### `duckdb_prepared_arrow_schema`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.9.0.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Fetch the internal arrow schema from the prepared statement. Remember to call release on the respective
ArrowSchema object.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_prepared_arrow_schema(
  duckdb_prepared_statement prepared,
  duckdb_arrow_schema *out_schema
);
```

##### Parameters

* `prepared`: The prepared statement to fetch the schema from.
* `out_schema`: The output schema. (out)

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure.

<br>

#### `duckdb_result_arrow_array`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.10.0.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Convert a data chunk into an arrow struct array. Remember to call release on the respective
ArrowArray object.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_result_arrow_array(
  duckdb_result result,
  duckdb_data_chunk chunk,
  duckdb_arrow_array *out_array
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result object the data chunk have been fetched from.
* `chunk`: The data chunk to convert.
* `out_array`: The output array. (out)

<br>

#### `duckdb_query_arrow_array`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Fetch an internal arrow struct array from the arrow result. Remember to call release on the respective
ArrowArray object.

This function can be called multiple time to get next chunks, which will free the previous out_array.
So consume the out_array before calling this function again.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_query_arrow_array(
  duckdb_arrow result,
  duckdb_arrow_array *out_array
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result to fetch the array from.
* `out_array`: The output array. (out)

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure.

<br>

#### `duckdb_arrow_column_count`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Returns the number of columns present in the arrow result object.

##### Syntax

```c
idx_t duckdb_arrow_column_count(
  duckdb_arrow result
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result object.

##### Return Value

The number of columns present in the result object.

<br>

#### `duckdb_arrow_row_count`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Returns the number of rows present in the arrow result object.

##### Syntax

```c
idx_t duckdb_arrow_row_count(
  duckdb_arrow result
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result object.

##### Return Value

The number of rows present in the result object.

<br>

#### `duckdb_arrow_rows_changed`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Returns the number of rows changed by the query stored in the arrow result. This is relevant only for
INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE queries. For other queries the rows_changed will be 0.

##### Syntax

```c
idx_t duckdb_arrow_rows_changed(
  duckdb_arrow result
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result object.

##### Return Value

The number of rows changed.

<br>

#### `duckdb_query_arrow_error`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

 Returns the error message contained within the result. The error is only set if `duckdb_query_arrow` returns
`DuckDBError`.

The error message should not be freed. It will be de-allocated when `duckdb_destroy_arrow` is called.

##### Syntax

```c
const char *duckdb_query_arrow_error(
  duckdb_arrow result
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result object to fetch the error from.

##### Return Value

The error of the result.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_arrow`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Closes the result and de-allocates all memory allocated for the arrow result.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_arrow(
  duckdb_arrow *result
);
```

##### Parameters

* `result`: The result to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_arrow_stream`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.10.0.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Releases the arrow array stream and de-allocates its memory.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_arrow_stream(
  duckdb_arrow_stream *stream_p
);
```

##### Parameters

* `stream_p`: The arrow array stream to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_execute_prepared_arrow`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.2.8.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Executes the prepared statement with the given bound parameters, and returns an arrow query result.
Note that after running `duckdb_execute_prepared_arrow`, `duckdb_destroy_arrow` must be called on the result object.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_execute_prepared_arrow(
  duckdb_prepared_statement prepared_statement,
  duckdb_arrow *out_result
);
```

##### Parameters

* `prepared_statement`: The prepared statement to execute.
* `out_result`: The query result. (out)

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure.

<br>

#### `duckdb_arrow_scan`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.9.0.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Scans the Arrow stream and creates a view with the given name.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_arrow_scan(
  duckdb_connection connection,
  const char *table_name,
  duckdb_arrow_stream arrow
);
```

##### Parameters

* `connection`: The connection on which to execute the scan.
* `table_name`: Name of the temporary view to create.
* `arrow`: Arrow stream wrapper.

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure.

<br>

#### `duckdb_arrow_array_scan`

> Deprecated Deprecated since 1.0.0, stable since 0.9.0.

This method is scheduled for removal in a future release.

Scans the Arrow array and creates a view with the given name.
Note that after running `duckdb_arrow_array_scan`, `duckdb_destroy_arrow_stream` must be called on the out stream.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_arrow_array_scan(
  duckdb_connection connection,
  const char *table_name,
  duckdb_arrow_schema arrow_schema,
  duckdb_arrow_array arrow_array,
  duckdb_arrow_stream *out_stream
);
```

##### Parameters

* `connection`: The connection on which to execute the scan.
* `table_name`: Name of the temporary view to create.
* `arrow_schema`: Arrow schema wrapper.
* `arrow_array`: Arrow array wrapper.
* `out_stream`: Output array stream that wraps around the passed schema, for releasing/deleting once done. (out)

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure.

<br>

### Expression Interface

<div class="language-c highlighter-rouge"><div class="highlight"><pre class="highlight"><code><span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_expression"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_expression</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_expression</span> *<span class="nv">expr</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_logical_type</span> <a href="#duckdb_expression_return_type"><span class="nf">duckdb_expression_return_type</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_expression</span> <span class="nv">expr</span>);
<span class="kt">bool</span> <a href="#duckdb_expression_is_foldable"><span class="nf">duckdb_expression_is_foldable</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_expression</span> <span class="nv">expr</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_error_data</span> <a href="#duckdb_expression_fold"><span class="nf">duckdb_expression_fold</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_client_context</span> <span class="nv">context</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_expression</span> <span class="nv">expr</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_value</span> *<span class="nv">out_value</span>);
</code></pre></div></div>

#### `duckdb_destroy_expression`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the expression and de-allocates its memory.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_expression(
  duckdb_expression *expr
);
```

##### Parameters

* `expr`: A pointer to the expression.

<br>

#### `duckdb_expression_return_type`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Returns the return type of an expression.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_logical_type duckdb_expression_return_type(
  duckdb_expression expr
);
```

##### Parameters

* `expr`: The expression.

##### Return Value

The return type. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_logical_type`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_expression_is_foldable`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Returns whether the expression is foldable into a value or not.

##### Syntax

```c
bool duckdb_expression_is_foldable(
  duckdb_expression expr
);
```

##### Parameters

* `expr`: The expression.

##### Return Value

True, if the expression is foldable, else false.

<br>

#### `duckdb_expression_fold`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Folds an expression creating a folded value.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_error_data duckdb_expression_fold(
  duckdb_client_context context,
  duckdb_expression expr,
  duckdb_value *out_value
);
```

##### Parameters

* `context`: The client context.
* `expr`: The expression. Must be foldable.
* `out_value`: The folded value, if folding was successful. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_value`. (out)

##### Return Value

The error data. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_error_data`.

<br>

### Threading Information

<div class="language-c highlighter-rouge"><div class="highlight"><pre class="highlight"><code><span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_execute_tasks"><span class="nf">duckdb_execute_tasks</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_database</span> <span class="nv">database</span>, <span class="kt">idx_t</span> <span class="nv">max_tasks</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_task_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_create_task_state"><span class="nf">duckdb_create_task_state</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_database</span> <span class="nv">database</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_execute_tasks_state"><span class="nf">duckdb_execute_tasks_state</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_task_state</span> <span class="nv">state</span>);
<span class="kt">idx_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_execute_n_tasks_state"><span class="nf">duckdb_execute_n_tasks_state</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_task_state</span> <span class="nv">state</span>, <span class="kt">idx_t</span> <span class="nv">max_tasks</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_finish_execution"><span class="nf">duckdb_finish_execution</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_task_state</span> <span class="nv">state</span>);
<span class="kt">bool</span> <a href="#duckdb_task_state_is_finished"><span class="nf">duckdb_task_state_is_finished</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_task_state</span> <span class="nv">state</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_task_state"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_task_state</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_task_state</span> <span class="nv">state</span>);
<span class="kt">bool</span> <a href="#duckdb_execution_is_finished"><span class="nf">duckdb_execution_is_finished</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_connection</span> <span class="nv">con</span>);
</code></pre></div></div>

#### `duckdb_execute_tasks`

Stable since 0.4.0.

Execute DuckDB tasks on this thread.

Will return after `max_tasks` have been executed, or if there are no more tasks present.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_execute_tasks(
  duckdb_database database,
  idx_t max_tasks
);
```

##### Parameters

* `database`: The database object to execute tasks for
* `max_tasks`: The maximum amount of tasks to execute

<br>

#### `duckdb_create_task_state`

Stable since 0.5.0.

Creates a task state that can be used with duckdb_execute_tasks_state to execute tasks until
`duckdb_finish_execution` is called on the state.

`duckdb_destroy_state` must be called on the result.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_task_state duckdb_create_task_state(
  duckdb_database database
);
```

##### Parameters

* `database`: The database object to create the task state for

##### Return Value

The task state that can be used with duckdb_execute_tasks_state.

<br>

#### `duckdb_execute_tasks_state`

Stable since 0.5.0.

Execute DuckDB tasks on this thread.

The thread will keep on executing tasks forever, until duckdb_finish_execution is called on the state.
Multiple threads can share the same duckdb_task_state.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_execute_tasks_state(
  duckdb_task_state state
);
```

##### Parameters

* `state`: The task state of the executor

<br>

#### `duckdb_execute_n_tasks_state`

Stable since 0.5.0.

Execute DuckDB tasks on this thread.

The thread will keep on executing tasks until either duckdb_finish_execution is called on the state,
max_tasks tasks have been executed or there are no more tasks to be executed.

Multiple threads can share the same duckdb_task_state.

##### Syntax

```c
idx_t duckdb_execute_n_tasks_state(
  duckdb_task_state state,
  idx_t max_tasks
);
```

##### Parameters

* `state`: The task state of the executor
* `max_tasks`: The maximum amount of tasks to execute

##### Return Value

The amount of tasks that have actually been executed

<br>

#### `duckdb_finish_execution`

Stable since 0.5.0.

Finish execution on a specific task.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_finish_execution(
  duckdb_task_state state
);
```

##### Parameters

* `state`: The task state to finish execution

<br>

#### `duckdb_task_state_is_finished`

Stable since 0.5.0.

Check if the provided duckdb_task_state has finished execution

##### Syntax

```c
bool duckdb_task_state_is_finished(
  duckdb_task_state state
);
```

##### Parameters

* `state`: The task state to inspect

##### Return Value

Whether or not duckdb_finish_execution has been called on the task state

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_task_state`

Stable since 0.5.0.

Destroys the task state returned from duckdb_create_task_state.

Note that this should not be called while there is an active duckdb_execute_tasks_state running
on the task state.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_task_state(
  duckdb_task_state state
);
```

##### Parameters

* `state`: The task state to clean up

<br>

#### `duckdb_execution_is_finished`

Stable since 0.7.0.

Returns true if the execution of the current query is finished.

##### Syntax

```c
bool duckdb_execution_is_finished(
  duckdb_connection con
);
```

##### Parameters

* `con`: The connection on which to check

##### Return Value

`bool`

<br>

### Catalog Interface

<div class="language-c highlighter-rouge"><div class="highlight"><pre class="highlight"><code><span class="kt">duckdb_catalog</span> <a href="#duckdb_client_context_get_catalog"><span class="nf">duckdb_client_context_get_catalog</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_client_context</span> <span class="nv">context</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<span class="nv">catalog_name</span>);
<span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<a href="#duckdb_catalog_get_type_name"><span class="nf">duckdb_catalog_get_type_name</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog</span> <span class="nv">catalog</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog_entry</span> <a href="#duckdb_catalog_get_entry"><span class="nf">duckdb_catalog_get_entry</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog</span> <span class="nv">catalog</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_client_context</span> <span class="nv">context</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_catalog_entry_type</span> <span class="nv">entry_type</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<span class="nv">schema_name</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<span class="nv">entry_name</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_catalog"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_catalog</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog</span> *<span class="nv">catalog</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog_entry_type</span> <a href="#duckdb_catalog_entry_get_type"><span class="nf">duckdb_catalog_entry_get_type</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog_entry</span> <span class="nv">entry</span>);
<span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<a href="#duckdb_catalog_entry_get_name"><span class="nf">duckdb_catalog_entry_get_name</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog_entry</span> <span class="nv">entry</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_catalog_entry"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_catalog_entry</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_catalog_entry</span> *<span class="nv">entry</span>);
</code></pre></div></div>

#### `duckdb_client_context_get_catalog`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieve a database catalog instance by name.
This function can only be called from within the context of an active transaction, e.g. during execution of a registered function callback. Otherwise returns `nullptr`.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_catalog duckdb_client_context_get_catalog(
  duckdb_client_context context,
  const char *catalog_name
);
```

##### Parameters

* `context`: The client context.
* `catalog_name`: The name of the catalog.

##### Return Value

The resulting catalog instance, or `nullptr` if called from outside an active transaction or if a catalog with the specified name does not exist. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_catalog`

<br>

#### `duckdb_catalog_get_type_name`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieve the “type name” of the given catalog.
E.g. for a DuckDB database, this returns 'duckdb'.
The returned string is owned by the catalog and remains valid until the catalog is destroyed.

##### Syntax

```c
const char *duckdb_catalog_get_type_name(
  duckdb_catalog catalog
);
```

##### Parameters

* `catalog`: The catalog.

##### Return Value

The type name of the catalog.

<br>

#### `duckdb_catalog_get_entry`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieve a catalog entry from the given catalog by type, schema name and entry name.
The returned catalog entry remains valid for the duration of the current transaction.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_catalog_entry duckdb_catalog_get_entry(
  duckdb_catalog catalog,
  duckdb_client_context context,
  duckdb_catalog_entry_type entry_type,
  const char *schema_name,
  const char *entry_name
);
```

##### Parameters

* `catalog`: The catalog.
* `context`: The client context.
* `entry_type`: The type of the catalog entry to retrieve.
* `schema_name`: The schema name of the catalog entry.
* `entry_name`: The name of the catalog entry.

##### Return Value

The resulting catalog entry, or `nullptr` if no such entry exists. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_catalog_entry`. Remains valid for the duration of the current transaction.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_catalog`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the given catalog instance.

Note that this does not actually “drop” the contents of the catalog; it merely frees the C API handle.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_catalog(
  duckdb_catalog *catalog
);
```

##### Parameters

* `catalog`: The catalog instance to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_catalog_entry_get_type`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Get the type of the given catalog entry.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_catalog_entry_type duckdb_catalog_entry_get_type(
  duckdb_catalog_entry entry
);
```

##### Parameters

* `entry`: The catalog entry.

##### Return Value

The type of the catalog entry.

<br>

#### `duckdb_catalog_entry_get_name`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Get the name of the given catalog entry.

##### Syntax

```c
const char *duckdb_catalog_entry_get_name(
  duckdb_catalog_entry entry
);
```

##### Parameters

* `entry`: The catalog entry.

##### Return Value

The name of the catalog entry. The returned string is owned by the catalog entry and remains valid until the catalog entry is destroyed.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_catalog_entry`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the given catalog entry instance.

Note that this does not actually “drop” the catalog entry from the database catalog; it merely frees the C API handle.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_catalog_entry(
  duckdb_catalog_entry *entry
);
```

##### Parameters

* `entry`: The catalog entry instance to destroy.

<br>

### File System Interface

<div class="language-c highlighter-rouge"><div class="highlight"><pre class="highlight"><code><span class="kt">duckdb_file_system</span> <a href="#duckdb_client_context_get_file_system"><span class="nf">duckdb_client_context_get_file_system</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_client_context</span> <span class="nv">context</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_file_system"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_file_system</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_system</span> *<span class="nv">file_system</span>);

<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_system_open"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_system_open</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_system</span> <span class="nv">file_system</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<span class="nv">path</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_file_open_options</span> <span class="nv">options</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> *<span class="nv">out_file</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_file_open_options</span> <a href="#duckdb_create_file_open_options"><span class="nf">duckdb_create_file_open_options</span></a>(<span class="kt">void</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_open_options_set_flag"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_open_options_set_flag</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_open_options</span> <span class="nv">options</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_file_flag</span> <span class="nv">flag</span>, <span class="kt">bool</span> <span class="nv">value</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_file_open_options"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_file_open_options</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_open_options</span> *<span class="nv">options</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_file_handle"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_file_handle</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> *<span class="nv">file_handle</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_error_data</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_error_data"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_error_data</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>);
<span class="kt">int64_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_read"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_read</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>, <span class="kt">void</span> *<span class="nv">buffer</span>, <span class="kt">int64_t</span> <span class="nv">size</span>);
<span class="kt">int64_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_write"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_write</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">void</span> *<span class="nv">buffer</span>, <span class="kt">int64_t</span> <span class="nv">size</span>);
<span class="kt">int64_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_tell"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_tell</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>);
<span class="kt">int64_t</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_size"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_size</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_seek"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_seek</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>, <span class="kt">int64_t</span> <span class="nv">position</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_sync"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_sync</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_file_handle_close"><span class="nf">duckdb_file_handle_close</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_file_handle</span> <span class="nv">file_handle</span>);
</code></pre></div></div>

#### `duckdb_client_context_get_file_system`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Get a file system instance associated with the given client context.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_file_system duckdb_client_context_get_file_system(
  duckdb_client_context context
);
```

##### Parameters

* `context`: The client context.

##### Return Value

The resulting file system instance. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_file_system`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_file_system`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the given file system instance.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_file_system(
  duckdb_file_system *file_system
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_system`: The file system instance to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_system_error_data`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the last error that occurred on the given file system instance.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_error_data duckdb_file_system_error_data(
  duckdb_file_system file_system
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_system`: The file system instance.

##### Return Value

The error data.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_system_open`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Opens a file at the given path with the specified options.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_file_system_open(
  duckdb_file_system file_system,
  const char *path,
  duckdb_file_open_options options,
  duckdb_file_handle *out_file
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_system`: The file system instance.
* `path`: The path to the file.
* `options`: The file open options specifying how to open the file.
* `out_file`: The resulting file handle instance, or `nullptr` if the open failed. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_file_handle`. (out)

##### Return Value

Whether the operation was successful. If not, the error data can be retrieved using `duckdb_file_system_error_data`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_create_file_open_options`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Creates a new file open options instance with blank settings.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_file_open_options duckdb_create_file_open_options(void);
```

##### Return Value

The new file open options instance. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_file_open_options`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_open_options_set_flag`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets a specific flag in the file open options.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_file_open_options_set_flag(
  duckdb_file_open_options options,
  duckdb_file_flag flag,
  bool value
);
```

##### Parameters

* `options`: The file open options instance.
* `flag`: The flag to set (e.g., read, write).
* `value`: If the flag is enabled or disabled.

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` if the flag is unrecognized or unsupported by this version of DuckDB.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_file_open_options`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the given file open options instance.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_file_open_options(
  duckdb_file_open_options *options
);
```

##### Parameters

* `options`: The file open options instance to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_file_handle`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys the given file handle and deallocates all associated resources.
This will also close the file if it is still open.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_file_handle(
  duckdb_file_handle *file_handle
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_error_data`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Retrieves the last error that occurred on the given file handle.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_error_data duckdb_file_handle_error_data(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle.

##### Return Value

The error data. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_error_data`

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_read`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Reads data from the file into the buffer.

##### Syntax

```c
int64_t duckdb_file_handle_read(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle,
  void *buffer,
  int64_t size
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to read from.
* `buffer`: The buffer to read data into.
* `size`: The number of bytes to read.

##### Return Value

The number of bytes actually read, or negative on error.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_write`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Writes data from the buffer to the file.

##### Syntax

```c
int64_t duckdb_file_handle_write(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle,
  const void *buffer,
  int64_t size
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to write to.
* `buffer`: The buffer containing data to write.
* `size`: The number of bytes to write.

##### Return Value

The number of bytes actually written, or negative on error.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_tell`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Tells the current position in the file.

##### Syntax

```c
int64_t duckdb_file_handle_tell(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to tell the position of.

##### Return Value

The current position in the file, or negative on error.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_size`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Gets the size of the file.

##### Syntax

```c
int64_t duckdb_file_handle_size(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to get the size of.

##### Return Value

The size of the file in bytes, or negative on error.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_seek`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Seeks to a specific position in the file.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_file_handle_seek(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle,
  int64_t position
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to seek in.

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure. If unsuccessful, the error data can be retrieved using `duckdb_file_handle_error_data`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_sync`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Synchronizes the file's state with the underlying storage.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_file_handle_sync(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to synchronize.

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure. If unsuccessful, the error data can be retrieved using `duckdb_file_handle_error_data`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_file_handle_close`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Closes the given file handle.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_file_handle_close(
  duckdb_file_handle file_handle
);
```

##### Parameters

* `file_handle`: The file handle to close.

##### Return Value

`DuckDBSuccess` on success or `DuckDBError` on failure. If unsuccessful, the error data can be retrieved using `duckdb_file_handle_error_data`.

<br>

### Log Storage

<div class="language-c highlighter-rouge"><div class="highlight"><pre class="highlight"><code><span class="kt">duckdb_log_storage</span> <a href="#duckdb_create_log_storage"><span class="nf">duckdb_create_log_storage</span></a>(<span class="kt">void</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_destroy_log_storage"><span class="nf">duckdb_destroy_log_storage</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_log_storage</span> *<span class="nv">log_storage</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_log_storage_set_write_log_entry"><span class="nf">duckdb_log_storage_set_write_log_entry</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_log_storage</span> <span class="nv">log_storage</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_logger_write_log_entry_t</span> <span class="nv">function</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_log_storage_set_extra_data"><span class="nf">duckdb_log_storage_set_extra_data</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_log_storage</span> <span class="nv">log_storage</span>, <span class="kt">void</span> *<span class="nv">extra_data</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_delete_callback_t</span> <span class="nv">delete_callback</span>);
<span class="kt">void</span> <a href="#duckdb_log_storage_set_name"><span class="nf">duckdb_log_storage_set_name</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_log_storage</span> <span class="nv">log_storage</span>, <span class="kt">const</span> <span class="kt">char</span> *<span class="nv">name</span>);
<span class="kt">duckdb_state</span> <a href="#duckdb_register_log_storage"><span class="nf">duckdb_register_log_storage</span></a>(<span class="kt">duckdb_database</span> <span class="nv">database</span>, <span class="kt">duckdb_log_storage</span> <span class="nv">log_storage</span>);
</code></pre></div></div>

#### `duckdb_create_log_storage`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Creates a new log storage object.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_log_storage duckdb_create_log_storage(void);
```

##### Return Value

A log storage object. Must be destroyed with `duckdb_destroy_log_storage`.

<br>

#### `duckdb_destroy_log_storage`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Destroys a log storage object.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_destroy_log_storage(
  duckdb_log_storage *log_storage
);
```

##### Parameters

* `log_storage`: The log storage object to destroy.

<br>

#### `duckdb_log_storage_set_write_log_entry`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the callback function for writing log entries.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_log_storage_set_write_log_entry(
  duckdb_log_storage log_storage,
  duckdb_logger_write_log_entry_t function
);
```

##### Parameters

* `log_storage`: The log storage object.
* `function`: The function to call.

<br>

#### `duckdb_log_storage_set_extra_data`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the extra data of the custom log storage.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_log_storage_set_extra_data(
  duckdb_log_storage log_storage,
  void *extra_data,
  duckdb_delete_callback_t delete_callback
);
```

##### Parameters

* `log_storage`: The log storage object.
* `extra_data`: The extra data that is passed back into the callbacks.

<br>

#### `duckdb_log_storage_set_name`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Sets the name of the log storage.

##### Syntax

```c
void duckdb_log_storage_set_name(
  duckdb_log_storage log_storage,
  const char *name
);
```

##### Parameters

* `log_storage`: The log storage object.
* `name`: The name of the log storage.

<br>

#### `duckdb_register_log_storage`

Stable since 1.5.6.

Registers a custom log storage for the logger.

##### Syntax

```c
duckdb_state duckdb_register_log_storage(
  duckdb_database database,
  duckdb_log_storage log_storage
);
```

##### Parameters

* `database`: A database object.
* `log_storage`: The log storage object.

##### Return Value

Whether the registration was successful.

<br>
